- Key content (8 decision-relevant lines):
  - The CMR engine evaluates empirical and theoretical claims against a unified library of mechanistic architectural templates. It bridges empirical observations (e.g., "high ceilings reduce divergent thi
  - Templates are authored as JSON files containing canonical variables, mechanistic explanations, and causal paths. The engine recognizes two generations of template schema.
  - Gen-1 templates rely on standalone `causal_links` arrays to define direct independent variable (IV) to dependent variable (DV) mappings.
  - Gen-2 templates employ multi-step `mechanism_chain` arrays to represent intricate theoretical chains.
  - - **`mechanism_chain`**: Sequential array of steps tracing the exact metabolic or cognitive pathway.

- Key content (10 decision-relevant lines):
  - | **CONSTITUTIVE** | 0.75 | The architectural feature IS the mechanism (e.g., window area IS daylight exposure) | Assigned to any parameter that requires a mediating neural step |
  - | **MECHANISM** | 0.60 | A complete neural/physiological causal pathway has been traced and experimentally tested | The pathway includes an un-tested inferential step |

  - | **FUNCTIONAL** | 0.50 | Same function served but via a different mechanism (functional analogy) | Used when data exist for a MECHANISM or EMPIRICAL assignment |
  - | **CAPACITY** | 0.45 | System has the capacity but mechanism is unspecified | Used for a parameter where empirical data exist |

### docs/Non_Empirical_Web_Integration_Spec_V1.0.md
- Modified: 2026-02-22T23:15:50.049174
- Size: 50,148 bytes
- Paper coverage: **NOT COVERED**
- Key content (10 decision-relevant lines):
  - Article Eater has 15 extraction template families with detailed schemas specifying what to extract from each paper type. It also has a web-of-belief architecture with typed nodes, typed edges, coheren
  - The consequence: approximately half of processed PDFs produce no usable claims. The extraction pipeline was designed around an implicit model — paper = empirical study = {Methods, Results, causal clai
  - A **review paper** contributes something categorically different: it proposes inferential structure. "Finding A from Lab X, Finding B from Lab Y, and Finding C from Lab Z — which looked unrelated — ar
  - A **theoretical paper** contributes proposed causal architectures — the templates against which empirical findings are evaluated. Without these, the BN has nodes but no principled way to determine edg
  - A **methodological critique** contributes validity assessments that propagate across the web, downgrading the confidence of every study using the criticized method.

  - But there is a conspicuous gap. The entire framework, if left at the implicit level, cannot explain why the *same physical space* produces radically different psychological responses depending on what
  - This is the problem that Activity Space (Kirsh, 2026) was designed to address, and it is the single most important theoretical addition the Goldilocks framework needs.

  - The Article Eater template corpus is an AI-assisted evidence synthesis covering architecture-wellbeing relationships. It contains 73+ mechanistic templates organized across 10 architectural domains, p
  - Each panel convenes a group of 6–15 named researchers as virtual participants. The panel format follows a structured sequence: position statements, moderated debates on contested claims, consensus cal
  - **The panels are AI-generated evidence syntheses, not transcripts of actual expert consultations.** No named researcher was contacted, consulted, or asked to endorse the positions attributed to them. 
  - **Category A — Direct from published work.** The claim is stated or directly supported in the researcher's published papers. These claims carry standard APA citations and can be verified against the c
  - **Category B — Consistent extrapolation.** The claim extends the researcher's published work in a direction consistent with their research program, but the specific parameter estimate or application c

  - **Deepening D1: Multi-Channel Interaction**. CREA2 specifies three pathways to divergent thinking — (A) disfluency via moderate noise 65–75 dB, (B) spaciousness via ceiling R_h 0.35–0.50 + dim 150 lux

  - **Deepening D3: Walking Path Parameters**. CREA3 specifies that walking promotes incubation (d ≈ 0.8 for divergent thinking) but does not parameterize the path: optimal duration, complexity, threshold

  - The Article Eater project has built a substantial inventory of mechanistic templates — formalized accounts of how specific neural, perceptual, and cognitive mechanisms operate. These templates are org
  - But an expert who encounters a research finding does not think in terms of scientific disciplines. An expert encountering a paper on wood surfaces and stress reduction thinks in terms of **architectur
  - **Scheme 1 — Mechanism-First (existing).** Templates organized by the scientific mechanism they formalize. Entry point: "I know the mechanism; which architectural attributes does it apply to?"

  - | **Timothy Beatley** | University of Virginia | Biophilic cities and biophilic design at the urban scale. Has spent decades translating biophilia into city planning. Knows where the theory meets prac
  - | **Grant Hildebrand** | University of Washington (emeritus) | Prospect-refuge in architecture — his *Origins of Architectural Pleasure* (1999) is the most systematic application of Appleton's theory

  - I have analyzed the provided v16.0 "Article Eater" package [cite: 1-788]. A critical review of its specification reveals a conceptual conflation: the data model (specifically the rules table [cite: 55
  - This is a category error. A causal finding is an observable, testable relationship, whereas a theoretical mechanism is a non-observable construct proposed to explain that finding. The v16.0 model, by 
  - Competing Explanations: Where multiple, mutually exclusive mechanisms are proposed for the same finding.
  - Explanatory Hierarchy: How high-level theories (e.g., "Predictive Processing") relate to mid-level mechanisms (e.g., "Perceptual Fluency") and low-level processes (e.g., "Reduced Prediction Error").
  - True Connectivity: The v16.0 model's "connectivity" is limited to parent-child aggregation (e.g., cortisol is_a_part_of stress). The true connectivity in science lies in the many-to-many links between

### docs/v17_src/README_Package_Index_v17.md
- Modified: 2026-01-10T02:42:39.346931
- Size: 6,991 bytes
- Paper coverage: **NOT COVERED**
- Key content (10 decision-relevant lines):
  - Dual-Hierarchy Model (Findings & Mechanisms)
  - The Finding Hierarchy (The "What"): Models the empirical, causal, and aggregational relationships. (e.g., Micro-Finding: [Plants -> Cortisol ↓] aggregates into Meso-Finding: [Plants -> Stress Reductio
  - The Mechanism Hierarchy (The "Why"): A new, independent hierarchy that models the purely theoretical relationships between explanatory constructs. (e.g., Theory: [Predictive Processing] explains Mecha
  - The Explanation Link (The "Bridge"): A new many-to-many join table that connects a specific Finding to a specific Mechanism, with its own provenance.
  - Purpose: The new conceptual "bible." Explains the "What" (Findings), the "Why" (Mechanisms), and the "Bridge" (Links).
